claimed is held down and learns anti-Hebbian, so the winners write the act, not the OR of every reflex] and pruning [ISL_PRUNE=1: each population holds its destination with its gene grip, 16 to start, has one one time in four; under competition a branch writes only unclaimed words)) with sparse one-word codes on reading (ISL_ONE_WORD=8: every reading lifetime's glyphs, sounds and voice codes drawn voice's and the hand's codes, the sound the voice makes for each token, which the teacher speaks too, and the feel of each move drawn from one seed for the whole run, as anatomy is, so evolution can toward what is there and it counts down, and wrong at 0 it dies, so its input is free for a new birth; a new population has it one time in four) and reading's paired-associate rungs (ISL_PAIRS=1:
